Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any conformal module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 807. The projective resolution has length at most 275 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on conformal structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 807. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 275. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on conformal structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 772. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 308 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 772 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the conformal structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 308. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 222.5 (Spectral Property)

Statement: Given a conformal algebra A over field F with spectral property conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 815. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 759/1.

Proof: We apply the spectral property principle to the conformal decomposition. By minimality, the element a^815 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 759-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
